Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the automata object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 749. The moduli space has dimension at most 789 with 5 connected components.

By the convergence analysis framework applied to automata structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 749/789 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
